Wagoner","slug":"nick-wagoner","contributor":{"id":2313,"description":"Nick Wagoner"}}],"keywords":["news","NFL","San Francisco 49ers","Deebo Samuel Sr","Nick Wagoner"],"story":"<p>SANTA CLARA, Calif. -- The <a data-clubhouse-guid=\"985a261f-296c-c95c-32a5-addc4df75001\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/team/_/name/sf/san-francisco-49ers\">San Francisco 49ers</a> will be without yet another of their top receivers for the foreseeable future after <a data-player-guid=\"7f300cb7-f0a1-a43a-d0b0-f8a57bf04eb3\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/3126486/deebo-samuel-sr\">Deebo Samuel Sr</a>. suffered a hamstring injury in Sunday's win against the <a data-clubhouse-guid=\"3767a23c-a17d-294f-288a-9086ad1f0680\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/team/_/name/sea/seattle-seahawks\">Seattle Seahawks</a>.</p>\n<p>After further testing on Samuel's right hamstring Monday, Niners coach Kyle Shanahan said that the injury \"got him pretty good\" and that the receiver is \"going to miss some time.\" It's unclear exactly how much time Samuel will miss, but the Niners know they will be without him for at least the next couple of games and likely a bit longer.</p>\n<p>The same is true of backup linebacker <a data-player-guid=\"422f52b2-b855-2040-aaae-cc12bc8ebe2a\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4241807/garret-wallow\">Garret Wallow</a>, who also suffered a hamstring injury against the Seahawks. Shanahan, while noting that the injury is \"not quite as bad\" as Samuel's, said Wallow will miss \"at least a couple of weeks.\" </p>\n<p><alsosee></p>\n<p>Samuel left Sunday's 32-29 win against Seattle with 5:06 left in the third quarter. Although he'd been favoring the right leg earlier, Samuel didn't exit for good until he was tackled by Seahawks linebacker <a data-player-guid=\"20397853-b67b-1668-9e63-7897433e6a57\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4428659/drake-thomas\">Drake Thomas</a> on the left sideline.</p>\n<p>As Samuel went to the ground, he landed on the ball and then lost it. As he attempted to recover it, Samuel couldn't reel it back in and then stayed down.</p>\n<p>The Niners medical staff ran out to help Samuel, who took a few minutes to get up and slowly make his way back to the sideline. The 49ers ruled out Samuel's potential return almost immediately.</p>\n<p>The 49ers are down four of the top five wideouts they hoped to have entering the season. <a data-player-guid=\"87c807fb-79fe-e421-02d2-6536bbe0618f\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4428209/ricky-pearsall\">Ricky Pearsall</a> (knee), <a data-player-guid=\"125416d3-0dcd-38cf-b093-b2c04574f8d5\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4710714/dezhaun-stribling\">De'Zhaun Stribling</a> (ankle) and <a data-player-guid=\"31f80c0b-02ca-cf2f-2fc3-669604f71430\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/3043116/demarcus-robinson\">Demarcus Robinson</a> (ankle) are on injured reserve.</p>\n<p>Robinson is the closest to returning, though he's not eligible to open his 21-day practice window until after next Monday night's game against the <a data-clubhouse-guid=\"fbeaf73e-6d6c-cca4-0809-713d9d741f90\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/team/_/name/wsh/washington-commanders\">Washington Commanders</a>. He has been doing conditioning on a side field during practice in the past week.</p>\n<p>Veteran <a data-player-guid=\"0c22d637-c0d7-6bac-7333-dc3a992bdc0d\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/3895856/christian-kirk\">Christian Kirk</a> (calf) is on IR as well and eligible to return to practice, though Shanahan indicated that isn't likely to happen this week.</p>\n<p>The 49ers enter Week 6 with <a data-player-guid=\"5073fc58-91c1-4e3d-826d-af754f9f40c7\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/16737/mike-evans\">Mike Evans</a>, <a data-player-guid=\"280e4563-f82e-37a3-a9ea-fd1a0232346f\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4431466/jordan-watkins\">Jordan Watkins</a>, <a data-player-guid=\"e6108d57-ec3b-2e99-7260-781af58bb13c\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/16731/brandin-cooks\">Brandin Cooks</a> and <a data-player-guid=\"788f8622-3564-9d11-752d-1265e7c75c91\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4575665/jacob-cowing\">Jacob Cowing</a> as the available receivers on the roster. The team also has <a data-player-guid=\"9e6c0797-e699-37d4-8467-0927e77d5046\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4683114/will-pauling\">Will Pauling</a>, <a data-player-guid=\"490317ca-ee46-fbc3-7ae0-6eded1e9c14e\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4386544/xavier-smith\">Xavier Smith</a> and <a data-player-guid=\"cb36d1d4-f5fe-3a23-c747-95a4663d4370\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/3115928/malik-turner\">Malik Turner</a> on the practice squad.</p>\n<p>\"I think we have enough,\" Shanahan said. \"We've got three guys on practice squad also who can help. I mean, anytime you lose this number of guys, whether it's four guys or five guys, whatever it is, it's a huge challenge. But we do have guys in this building who can do it, and we'll always look.\"</p>\n<p>Wallow's injury in tandem with the one-game suspension for starting weakside linebacker <a data-player-guid=\"22bed4b5-a5ad-ccac-e96e-4ba5bd7d1173\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/3916903/dre-greenlaw\">Dre Greenlaw</a> complicates matters for the Niners at linebacker at least for next week's game.</p>\n<p>The Niners will lean on rookie <a data-player-guid=\"8b3fad41-b8cf-3320-905b-dc7f1a1dc254\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/5086161/jaden-dugger\">Jaden Dugger</a> with <a data-player-guid=\"62d72de9-f94c-8889-ae03-1f8c224a082c\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/3116097/luke-gifford\">Luke Gifford</a> and <a data-player-guid=\"9078d855-7620-9d34-1132-e9f477c39fd4\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4426698/tatum-bethune\">Tatum Bethune</a> offering depth, but they could need to dip into the practice squad for <a data-player-guid=\"9d74aa00-a67d-56d2-19f8-a0c2a302ac5f\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4570410/jalen-graham\">Jalen Graham</a> if Greenlaw's appeal is unsuccessful.</p>\n<p>Shanahan said linebacker <a data-player-guid=\"4d58785d-74ce-b921-b3c1-d3947e912f2f\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4573188/nick-martin\">Nick Martin</a>, who is on IR with a hamstring injury, is getting close and the team might look into opening his practice window this week.</p>\n<p>In better injury news for the 49ers, Shanahan said defensive end <a data-player-guid=\"6a5ec871-ae65-1fde-4664-f513284ad1ba\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4040605/nick-bosa\">Nick Bosa</a> (calf), fullback <a data-player-guid=\"b325648c-02db-e176-0357-6afed508ef50\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/16002/kyle-juszczyk\">Kyle Juszczyk</a> (knee) and defensive tackle <a data-player-guid=\"a48b6309-08b8-3436-a564-d65919c4f21c\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4693371/james-thompson-jr\">James Thompson Jr</a>. (ankle) could return this week.</p>\n<p>\"[They're] all kind of in the same boat,\" Shanahan said. \"They all got a chance, but definitely not saying they're good to go right now. We've got to continue to evaluate them throughout the week and it'll take all week, but hopefully they can get there.\"</p>\n\n\n\n\n","allowAMP":true,"allowAds":true,"allowComments":true,"allowCommerce":true,"allowContentReactions":true,"allowSearch":true,"byline":"Nick Wagoner","isLiveBlog":false,"links":{"api":{"self":{"href":"https://content.core.api.espn.com/v1/sports/news/50168446"}},"app":{"sportscenter":{"href":"sportscenter://x-callback-url/showStory?uid=50168446"}},"mobile":{"href":"http://m.espn.go.com/nfl/story?storyId=50168446"},"web":{"href":"https://www.espn.com/nfl/story/_/id/50168446/shanahan-49ers-wr-samuel-miss-hamstring-injury"}},"premium":false},{"id":50168293,"nowId":"1-50168293","contentKey":"50168293-1-5-1","dataSourceIdentifier":"e21c64e79da84","publishedkey":"50168293","type":"HeadlineNews","feedDisplayType":"Default","headline":"Sources: Falcons QB Michael Penix's rib injury not considered serious","description":"Falcons quarterback Michael Penix Jr.'s rib injury is not considered serious, sources told ESPN's Adam Schefter.","title":"Sources: Falcons QB Penix's rib injury not considered serious","linkText":"Sources: Falcons QB Penix's rib injury not considered serious","categorized":"2026-10-12T20:10:17Z","originallyPosted":"2026-10-12T19:36:13Z","lastModified":"2026-10-12T20:10:16Z","published":"2026-10-12T20:10:16Z","root":"nfl","section":"NFL","images":[{"dataSourceIdentifier":"c0a4b85c854c7","id":50168232,"type":"header","name":"Michael Penix  [1296x729]","credit":"Kevin C. data-clubhouse-guid=\"49fd392a-86fe-4df3-1b77-9bbfa18b2ad5\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/team/_/name/atl/atlanta-falcons\">Atlanta Falcons</a> quarterback <a data-player-guid=\"28022fa1-9050-eee1-5160-c77a6a379ec2\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4360423/michael-penix-jr\">Michael Penix Jr</a>. came out of Sunday's loss to the <a data-clubhouse-guid=\"38248455-3da4-84e4-8090-98b5cc38ffdc\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/team/_/name/bal/baltimore-ravens\">Baltimore Ravens</a> with a rib injury, but it is not considered serious, sources told ESPN's Adam Schefter on Monday.</p>\n<p>Penix, the sources said, might be able to return for this Sunday's game against the <a data-clubhouse-guid=\"19d73366-b5a5-cca2-a176-a2c024ce0bb3\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/team/_/name/chi/chicago-bears\">Chicago Bears</a>. But the team wants to be cautious and see him practice this week first before deciding on the next move.</p>\n<p>Falcons head coach Kevin Stefanski did not provide any health update on Penix in his news conference Monday. Stefanski said he did not want to go into details about the results of Penix's imaging and X-rays but should have more information by Wednesday.</p>\n<p><alsosee></p>\n<p>Penix was sacked by <a data-player-guid=\"120cd699-9f97-35a9-a175-c5661877ceea\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4690179/tavius-robinson\">Tavius Robinson</a> late in the fourth quarter of a tie game Sunday against the Ravens. The QB stayed down on the grass for a bit before coming off with a trainer. <a data-player-guid=\"3858bbe0-6348-e3ef-5d9f-2dd2acb46f44\" href=\"https://www.espn.com/nfl/player/_/id/4241479/tua-tagovailoa\">Tua Tagovailoa</a> then came in for a single snap before Penix reentered for what was the last play of the drive.</p>\n<p>When the game went into overtime, Penix did not come back in. He said if the Falcons got the ball first to start the period, he might have returned. But the pain got worse and worse as he got cold sitting on the bench and he was unable to get comfortable.</p>\n<p>The Ravens ended up winning 30-23. Tagovailoa threw a game-ending interception with 2:11 left in OT.</p>\n<p>The Falcons just got Penix back from ACL surgery in Week 3, and he helped turn around what was the worst offense in the NFL.
